PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

Here is the Sokoban problem to solve, enclosed in triple backticks:
'''
###
#.#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
#$#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
#@#
###

'''
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

Here is the Sokoban problem to solve, enclosed in triple backticks:
'''
###
#.#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
#$#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
#@#
###

'''
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain sokoban_corridor)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types position box player)

  (:predicates
    (player_at ?p - position)
    (box_at ?b - box ?p - position)
    (goal ?p - position)
    (adjacent_up ?below ?above - position)
    (empty ?p - position)
  )

  (:action walk_up
    :parameters (?from - position ?to - position)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?from)
      (adjacent_up ?from ?to)
      (empty ?to)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?from))
      (player_at ?to)
      (not (empty ?to))
      (empty ?from)
    )
  )

  (:action walk_down
    :parameters (?from - position ?to - position)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?from)
      (adjacent_up ?to ?from)
      (empty ?to)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?from))
      (player_at ?to)
      (not (empty ?to))
      (empty ?from)
    )
  )

  (:action push_up
    :parameters (?pfrom - position ?pbox - position ?pto - position)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?pfrom)
      (box_at box1 ?pbox)
      (adjacent_up ?pfrom ?pbox)
      (adjacent_up ?pbox ?pto)
      (empty ?pto)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (box_at box1 ?pbox))
      (box_at box1 ?pto)
      (not (player_at ?pfrom))
      (player_at ?pbox)
      (not (empty ?pto))
      (not (empty ?pbox))
      (empty ?pfrom)
    )
  )

  (:action push_down
    :parameters (?pfrom - position ?pbox - position ?pto - position)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?pfrom)
      (box_at box1 ?pbox)
      (adjacent_up ?pbox ?pfrom)
      (adjacent_up ?pto ?pbox)
      (empty ?pto)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (box_at box1 ?pbox))
      (box_at box1 ?pto)
      (not (player_at ?pfrom))
      (player_at ?pbox)
      (not (empty ?pto))
      (not (empty ?pbox))
      (empty ?pfrom)
    )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem sokoban_instance)
  (:domain sokoban_corridor)
  (:objects
    pos_1 pos_2 pos_3 pos_4 pos_5 pos_6 pos_7 pos_8 pos_9 pos_10
    pos_11 pos_12 pos_13 pos_14 pos_15 pos_16 pos_17 pos_18 pos_19 pos_20

    pos_91 pos_92 pos_93 pos_94 pos_95 pos_96 pos_97 pos_98 pos_99 pos_100
    pos_101 pos_102 pos_103 pos_104 pos_105 pos_106 pos_107 pos_108 pos_109 pos_110
    pos_111 pos_112 pos_113 pos_114 pos_115 pos_116 pos_117 pos_118 pos_119 pos_120

    pos_131 pos_132 pos_133 pos_134 pos_135 pos_136 pos_137 pos_138 pos_139 pos_140
    box1 - box

  )

  (:init
    ;; goal location (topmost walkable cell)
    (goal pos_1)

    ;; initial locations
    ;; box is located near the bottom, player directly below it
    (box_at box1 pos_139)

================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain sokoban_corridor)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types position box player)

  (:predicates
    (player_at ?p - position)
    (box_at ?b - box ?p - position)
    (goal ?p - position)
    (adjacent_up ?below ?above - position)
    (empty ?p - position)
  )

  (:action walk_up
    :parameters (?from - position ?to - position)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?from)
      (adjacent_up ?from ?to)
      (empty ?to)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?from))
      (player_at ?to)
      (not (empty ?to))
      (empty ?from)
    )
  )

  (:action walk_down
    :parameters (?from - position ?to - position)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?from)
      (adjacent_up ?to ?from)
      (empty ?to)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?from))
      (player_at ?to)
      (not (empty ?to))
      (empty ?from)
    )
  )

  (:action push_up
    :parameters (?pfrom - position ?pbox - position ?pto - position)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?pfrom)
      (box_at box1 ?pbox)
      (adjacent_up ?pfrom ?pbox)
      (adjacent_up ?pbox ?pto)
      (empty ?pto)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (box_at box1 ?pbox))
      (box_at box1 ?pto)
      (not (player_at ?pfrom))
      (player_at ?pbox)
      (not (empty ?pto))
      (not (empty ?pbox))
      (empty ?pfrom)
    )
  )

  (:action push_down
    :parameters (?pfrom - position ?pbox - position ?pto - position)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?pfrom)
      (box_at box1 ?pbox)
      (adjacent_up ?pbox ?pfrom)
      (adjacent_up ?pto ?pbox)
      (empty ?pto)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (box_at box1 ?pbox))
      (box_at box1 ?pto)
      (not (player_at ?pfrom))
      (player_at ?pbox)
      (not (empty ?pto))
      (not (empty ?pbox))
      (empty ?pfrom)
    )
  )
)

You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.

================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain sokoban_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types agent box cell)

  (:predicates
    (at_agent ?a - agent ?c - cell)
    (at_box ?b - box ?c - cell)
    (adjacent ?c1 - cell ?c2 - cell)
    (goal_cell ?c - cell)
  )

  ; move the agent into an adjacent empty cell (destination must not contain any box)
  (:action move
    :parameters (?a - agent ?from - cell ?to - cell ?b - box)
    :precondition (and
      (at_agent ?a ?from)
      (adjacent ?from ?to)
      (not (at_box ?b ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_agent ?a ?from))
      (at_agent ?a ?to)
    )
  )

  ; push the box from box_from to box_to; agent moves into box_from
  (:action push
    :parameters (?a - agent ?b - box ?agent_from - cell ?box_from - cell ?box_to - cell)
    :precondition (and
      (at_agent ?a ?agent_from)
      (at_box ?b ?box_from)
      (adjacent ?agent_from ?box_from)
      (adjacent ?box_from ?box_to)
      (not (at_box ?b ?box_to))
      (not (at_agent ?a ?box_to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_agent ?a ?agent_from))
      (at_agent ?a ?box_from)
      (not (at_box ?b ?box_from))
      (at_box ?b ?box_to)
    )
  )
)
</domain>

DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain sokoban_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types agent box cell)

  (:predicates
    (at_agent ?a - agent ?c - cell)
    (at_box ?b - box ?c - cell)
    (adjacent ?c1 - cell ?c2 - cell)
    (goal_cell ?c - cell)
  )

  ; move the agent into an adjacent empty cell (destination must not contain any box)
  (:action move
    :parameters (?a - agent ?from - cell ?to - cell ?b - box)
    :precondition (and
      (at_agent ?a ?from)
      (adjacent ?from ?to)
      (not (at_box ?b ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_agent ?a ?from))
      (at_agent ?a ?to)
    )
  )

  ; push the box from box_from to box_to; agent moves into box_from
  (:action push
    :parameters (?a - agent ?b - box ?agent_from - cell ?box_from - cell ?box_to - cell)
    :precondition (and
      (at_agent ?a ?agent_from)
      (at_box ?b ?box_from)
      (adjacent ?agent_from ?box_from)
      (adjacent ?box_from ?box_to)
      (not (at_box ?b ?box_to))
      (not (at_agent ?a ?box_to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at_agent ?a ?agent_from))
      (at_agent ?a ?box_from)
      (not (at_box ?b ?box_from))
      (at_box ?b ?box_to)
    )
  )
)
================================================================================

PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain sokoban_corridor)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types agent box position)
  (:predicates
    (at ?a - agent ?p - position)
    (box_at ?b - box ?p - position)
    (goal ?p - position)
    (next ?p1 - position ?p2 - position)
  )

  (:action move
    :parameters (?a - agent ?from - position ?to - position)
    :precondition (and
      (at ?a ?from)
      (next ?from ?to)
      (not (box_at box1 ?to))
      (not (at ?a ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at ?a ?from))
      (at ?a ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action push
    :parameters (?a - agent ?b - box ?from - position ?boxpos - position ?into - position)
    :precondition (and
      (at ?a ?from)
      (box_at ?b ?boxpos)
      (next ?from ?boxpos)
      (next ?boxpos ?into)
      (not (box_at ?b ?into))
      (not (at ?a ?into))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (box_at ?b ?boxpos))
      (box_at ?b ?into)
      (not (at ?a ?from))
      (at ?a ?boxpos)
    )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>

DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain sokoban_corridor)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types agent box position)
  (:predicates
    (at ?a - agent ?p - position)
    (box_at ?b - box ?p - position)
    (goal ?p - position)
    (next ?p1 - position ?p2 - position)
  )

  (:action move
    :parameters (?a - agent ?from - position ?to - position)
    :precondition (and
      (at ?a ?from)
      (next ?from ?to)
      (not (box_at box1 ?to))
      (not (at ?a ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at ?a ?from))
      (at ?a ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action push
    :parameters (?a - agent ?b - box ?from - position ?boxpos - position ?into - position)
    :precondition (and
      (at ?a ?from)
      (box_at ?b ?boxpos)
      (next ?from ?boxpos)
      (next ?boxpos ?into)
      (not (box_at ?b ?into))
      (not (at ?a ?into))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (box_at ?b ?boxpos))
      (box_at ?b ?into)
      (not (at ?a ?from))
      (at ?a ?boxpos)
    )
  )
)
================================================================================

161. t=160s: Push: agent1 at pos_3 pushes box1 from pos_2 into pos_1 (agent1 -> pos_2; box1 -> pos_1).
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

Here is the Sokoban problem to solve, enclosed in triple backticks:
'''
###
#@#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #

# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
#$#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #

# #

# #

###

'''
================================================================================

------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.

All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:

The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.

'''
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain sokoban_domain)
  (:requirements :typing :negative-preconditions :strips)
  (:types cell player box)
  (:predicates
    (cell ?c - cell)
    (player ?p - player)
    (box ?b - box)
    (adjacent ?a - cell ?b - cell)
    (player_at ?p - player ?c - cell)
    (box_at ?b - box ?c - cell)
    (goal_cell ?c - cell)
    (wall_left ?c - cell)
    (wall_right ?c - cell)
    (empty ?c - cell)
  )

  (:action move
    :parameters (?p - player ?from - cell ?to - cell)
    :precondition (and
      (player ?p)
      (player_at ?p ?from)
      (adjacent ?to ?from)
      (empty ?to)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?p ?from))
      (player_at ?p ?to)
      (not (empty ?to))
      (empty ?from)
    )
  )

  (:action push
    :parameters (?p - player ?b - box ?from - cell ?boxcell - cell ?to - cell)
    :precondition (and
      (player ?p)
      (box ?b)
      (player_at ?p ?from)
      (box_at ?b ?boxcell)
      (adjacent ?boxcell ?from)
      (adjacent ?to ?boxcell)
      (empty ?to)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (box_at ?b ?boxcell))
      (box_at ?b ?to)
      (not (player_at ?p ?from))
      (player_at ?p ?boxcell)
      (empty ?from)
      (not (empty ?to))
    )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem sokoban_problem)
  (:domain sokoban_domain)
  (:objects
    player1 - player
    box1 - box
    r1 r2 r3 r4 r5 r6 r7 r8 r9 r10
    r11 r12 r13 r14 r15 r16 r17 r18 r19 r20
    r21 r22 r23 r24 r25 r26 r27 r28 r29 r30
    r31 r32 r33 r34 r35 r36 r37 r38 r39 r40
    r41 r42 r43 r44 r45 r46 r47 r48 r49 r50
    r51 r52 r53 r54 r55 r56 r57 r58 r59 r60
    r61 r62 r63 r64 r65 r66 r67 r68 r69 r70
    r71 r72 r73 r74 r75 r76 r77 r78 r79 r80
    r81 r82 r83 r84 r85 r86 r87 r88 r89 r90
    r91 r92 r93 r94 r95 - cell
  )

  (:init
    ;; type facts
    (player player1)
    (box box1)
    ;; cells
    (cell r1) (cell r2) (cell r3) (cell r4) (cell r5)
    (cell r6) (cell r7) (cell r8) (cell r9) (cell r10)
    (cell r11) (cell r12) (cell r13) (cell r14) (cell r15)

    (cell r36) (cell r37) (cell r38) (cell r39) (cell r40)
    (cell r41) (cell r42) (cell r43) (cell r44) (cell r45)
    (cell r46) (cell r47) (cell r48) (cell r49) (cell r50)
    (cell r51) (cell r52) (cell r53) (cell r54) (cell r55)
    (cell r56) (cell r57) (cell r58) (cell r59) (cell r60)
    (cell r61) (cell r62) (cell r63) (cell r64) (cell r65)
    (cell r66) (cell r67) (cell r68) (cell r69) (cell r70)
    (cell r71) (cell r72) (cell r73) (cell r74) (cell r75)
    (cell r76) (cell r77) (cell r78) (cell r79) (cell r80)
    (cell r81) (cell r82) (cell r83) (cell r84) (cell r85)
    (cell r86) (cell r87) (cell r88) (cell r89) (cell r90)
    (cell r91) (cell r92) (cell r93) (cell r94) (cell r95)

    ;; adjacency chain (bidirectional)
    (adjacent r1 r2) (adjacent r2 r1)
    (adjacent r2 r3) (adjacent r3 r2)
    (adjacent r3 r4) (adjacent r4 r3)

</problem>
================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain sokoban_domain)
  (:requirements :typing :negative-preconditions :strips)
  (:types cell player box)
  (:predicates
    (cell ?c - cell)
    (player ?p - player)
    (box ?b - box)
    (adjacent ?a - cell ?b - cell)
    (player_at ?p - player ?c - cell)
    (box_at ?b - box ?c - cell)
    (goal_cell ?c - cell)
    (wall_left ?c - cell)
    (wall_right ?c - cell)
    (empty ?c - cell)
  )

  (:action move
    :parameters (?p - player ?from - cell ?to - cell)
    :precondition (and
      (player ?p)
      (player_at ?p ?from)
      (adjacent ?to ?from)
      (empty ?to)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?p ?from))
      (player_at ?p ?to)
      (not (empty ?to))
      (empty ?from)
    )
  )

  (:action push
    :parameters (?p - player ?b - box ?from - cell ?boxcell - cell ?to - cell)
    :precondition (and
      (player ?p)
      (box ?b)
      (player_at ?p ?from)
      (box_at ?b ?boxcell)
      (adjacent ?boxcell ?from)
      (adjacent ?to ?boxcell)
      (empty ?to)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (box_at ?b ?boxcell))
      (box_at ?b ?to)
      (not (player_at ?p ?from))
      (player_at ?p ?boxcell)
      (empty ?from)
      (not (empty ?to))
    )
  )
)
================================================================================

------------------------------

87. [t=00:01:27] Push: player1 pushes box1 from r89 to r90; player1 moves into r89.
88. [t=00:01:28] Push: player1 pushes box1 from r90 to r91; player1 moves into r90.
89. [t=00:01:29] Push: player1 pushes box1 from r91 to r92; player1 moves into r91.
90. [t=00:01:30] Push: player1 pushes box1 from r92 to r93; player1 moves into r92.
91. [t=00:01:31] Push: player1 pushes box1 from r93 to r94; player1 moves into r93.
92. [t=00:01:32] Push: player1 pushes box1 from r94 to r95; player1 moves into r94.
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

Here is the Sokoban problem to solve, enclosed in triple backticks:
'''
###
#.#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #

# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
#$#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #

#@#

'''
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''

. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.

================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain sokoban_jackofalltrades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types agent box position)
  (:predicates
    (at ?a - agent ?p - position)
    (box_at ?b - box ?p - position)
    (is_goal ?p - position)
    (empty ?p - position)
    (adj ?p1 - position ?p2 - position)
  )

  (:action move
    :parameters (?a - agent ?from - position ?to - position)
    :precondition (and (at ?a ?from) (adj ?from ?to) (empty ?to))
    :effect (and
             (not (at ?a ?from))
             (at ?a ?to)
             (empty ?from)
             (not (empty ?to))
            )
  )

  (:action push
    :parameters (?a - agent ?b - box ?agent_pos - position ?box_pos - position ?box_dest - position)
    :precondition (and
                    (at ?a ?agent_pos)
                    (box_at ?b ?box_pos)
                    (adj ?agent_pos ?box_pos)
                    (adj ?box_pos ?box_dest)
                    (empty ?box_dest)
                   )
    :effect (and
             (not (at ?a ?agent_pos))
             (at ?a ?box_pos)
             (not (box_at ?b ?box_pos))
             (box_at ?b ?box_dest)
             (empty ?agent_pos)
             (not (empty ?box_dest))
            )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>

================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain sokoban_jackofalltrades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types agent box position)
  (:predicates
    (at ?a - agent ?p - position)
    (box_at ?b - box ?p - position)
    (is_goal ?p - position)
    (empty ?p - position)
    (adj ?p1 - position ?p2 - position)
  )

  (:action move
    :parameters (?a - agent ?from - position ?to - position)
    :precondition (and (at ?a ?from) (adj ?from ?to) (empty ?to))
    :effect (and
             (not (at ?a ?from))
             (at ?a ?to)
             (empty ?from)
             (not (empty ?to))
            )
  )

  (:action push
    :parameters (?a - agent ?b - box ?agent_pos - position ?box_pos - position ?box_dest - position)
    :precondition (and
                    (at ?a ?agent_pos)
                    (box_at ?b ?box_pos)
                    (adj ?agent_pos ?box_pos)
                    (adj ?box_pos ?box_dest)
                    (empty ?box_dest)
                   )
    :effect (and
             (not (at ?a ?agent_pos))
             (at ?a ?box_pos)
             (not (box_at ?b ?box_pos))
             (box_at ?b ?box_dest)
             (empty ?agent_pos)
             (not (empty ?box_dest))
            )
  )
)
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------

  (:domain sokoban_jackofalltrades)
  (:objects
    player - agent

    p1 p2 p3 p4 p5 p6 p7 p8 p9 p10 p11 p12 p13 p14 p15 p16 p17 p18 p19 p20
